'' : 'none';\n        });\n      });\n      sel.addEventListener('click', e=>e.stopPropagation());\n      sel.addEventListener('mousedown', e=>e.stopPropagation());\n      sel.addEventListener('keydown', e=>e.stopPropagation());\n    });\n  }\n\n  renderStartups();\n\n  // ===== This Week's Research-Backed Startups (dynamic) =====\n  // Fetches data/weekly_digest/latest/startups.json (pushed by the weekly\n  // GitHub Actions workflow) and renders 7 dynamic startup cards reusing\n  // the same .startup-card styling as the static portfolio above.\n\n  const WEEKLY_JSON = 'data/weekly_digest/latest/startups.json';\n  const WEEKLY_POOL_JSON = 'data/weekly_digest/latest/all_articles.json';\n\n  function escapeHtml(s){\n    return String(s||'').replace(/[&<>\"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','\"':'&quot;',\"'\":'&#39;'}[c]));\n  }\n\n  function renderPaperBadge(paper){\n    if(!paper) return '<span style=\"color:var(--text-dim);font-size:.78rem\">paper unavailable</span>';\n    if(paper.source === 'unavailable' || paper.source === 'skipped'){\n      return `<span style=\"color:var(--text-dim);font-size:.78rem\">paper unavailable · <a href=\"${escapeHtml(paper.url)}\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" style=\"color:var(--text-muted);text-decoration:underline\">ScienceDaily</a></span>`;\n    }\n    const srcLabel = paper.source === 'arxiv' ? 'arXiv' : (paper.venue || 'paper');\n    const year = paper.year ? `(${escapeHtml(paper.year)})` : '';\n    const doiLink = paper.doi_url\n      ? `<a href=\"${escapeHtml(paper.doi_url)}\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" style=\"color:var(--accent-2);text-decoration:underline;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.78rem\">DOI:${escapeHtml(paper.doi.slice(0,28))}${paper.doi.length>28?'…':''}</a>`\n      : '';\n    return `<span style=\"font-size:.82rem\"><strong style=\"color:var(--tier2)\">${escapeHtml(srcLabel)}</strong> ${year}${doiLink ? ' · '+doiLink : ''}</span>`;\n  }\n\n  // ===== 7-paper picker (expandable abstracts) =====\n  function renderPaperPicker(papers){\n    if(!papers || !papers.length){\n      return '<div style=\"color:var(--text-dim);font-size:.82rem;padding:.5rem 0\">No papers attached.</div>';\n    }\n    return `\n      <div style=\"margin:0 0 .85rem;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:.4rem\">\n        ${papers.map((p,i)=>renderPaperCard(p,i)).join('')}\n      </div>\n    `;\n  }\n\n  // ===== Papers lookup dropdown (compact select-style) =====\n  // Renders a <select> + a stacked set of pre-rendered paper cards. Only the\n  // selected card is visible; the rest are display:none. Switching the select\n  // toggles which card is shown — no fetch, no rebuild, no flicker.\n  function renderPapersLookup(papers, cardIdx){\n    if(!papers || !papers.length){\n      return '<div style=\"color:var(--text-dim);font-size:.82rem;padding:.5rem 0\">No papers attached.</div>';\n    }\n\n    // Cap at 12 papers to keep DOM lightweight (the weekly digest typically has 5–7)\n    const visible = papers.slice(0, 12);\n    const ddId = `papers-dd-${cardIdx}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`;\n\n    const options = visible.map((p,i)=>{\n      const title = (p.title || '(untitled)').slice(0, 70);\n      const tag = p.is_seed ? '★ SEED' : 'related';\n      const venue = p.venue || p.source || '';\n      const year = p.year ? ` ${p.year}` : '';\n      const label = `${tag} · ${title}${venue ? ' · ' + venue : ''}${year}`;\n      return `<option value=\"${i}\">${escapeHtml(label)}</option>`;\n    }).join('');\n\n    const cardsHtml = visible.map((p,i)=>`\n      <div class=\"paper-lookup-card\" data-dd-id=\"${ddId}\" data-paper-idx=\"${i}\" style=\"${i===0?'':'display:none'}\">\n        ${renderPaperCard(p, i)}\n      </div>\n    `).join('');\n\n    return `\n      <div style=\"background:#0a0f1c;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:.7rem .85rem;margin-bottom:.5rem\">\n        <div style=\"display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem;margin-bottom:.55rem\">\n          <label for=\"${ddId}\" style=\"font-size:.74rem;color:var(--text-dim);font-weight:600;white-space:nowrap;letter-spacing:.04em;text-transform:uppercase\">Pick:</label>\n          <select id=\"${ddId}\" class=\"papers-lookup-select\"\n                  style=\"flex:1;background:var(--surface-2);border:1px solid var(--border-strong);color:var(--text);padding:.4rem .55rem;border-radius:6px;font-size:.8rem;font-family:inherit;min-width:0;cursor:pointer\">\n            ${options}\n          </select>\n          <span style=\"font-size:.72rem;color:var(--text-dim);font-family:var(--mono)\">${visible.length}/${papers.length}</span>\n        </div>\n        <div>${cardsHtml}</div>\n      </div>\n    `;\n  }\n\n  function renderPaperCard(paper, idx){\n    const isSeed = paper.is_seed;\n    const source = paper.source || 'unknown';\n    const title = paper.title || '(untitled)';\n    const venue = paper.venue || '';\n    const year = paper.year || '';\n    const authors = paper.authors || '';\n    const abstract = paper.abstract || '';\n    const tldr = paper.tldr || '';\n    const citationCount = paper.citation_count || 0;\n    const doiUrl = paper.doi_url || '';\n    const paperUrl = paper.url || doiUrl || '';\n    const pdfUrl = paper.open_access_pdf || '';\n    const arxivId = paper.arxiv_id || '';\n    const reason = paper.relevance_reason || '';\n\n    // Badge color by source\n    let badgeColor = 'var(--text-dim)';\n    let badgeText = source.toUpperCase();\n    if(source === 'crossref'){ badgeColor = 'var(--tier2)'; badgeText = 'CROSSREF'; }\n    else if(source === 'arxiv'){ badgeColor = 'var(--accent-2)'; badgeText = 'ARXIV'; }\n    else if(source === 'sciencedaily'){ badgeColor = 'var(--tier3)'; badgeText = 'SCIDAILY'; }\n\n    // Seed gets a star\n    const seedBadge = isSeed\n      ? `<span style=\"background:var(--tier1);color:#000;padding:1px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-size:.7rem;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.04em\">★ SEED</span>`\n      : `<span style=\"background:rgba(255,255,255,.06);color:var(--text-muted);padding:1px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-size:.7rem;font-weight:600\">RELATED</span>`;\n\n    const sourceBadge = `<span style=\"background:rgba(96,165,250,.12);color:${badgeColor};padding:1px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-size:.7rem;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.04em\">${badgeText}</span>`;\n\n    // Citation count badge\n    const citeBadge = citationCount > 0\n      ? `<span style=\"background:rgba(52,211,153,.12);color:var(--tier2);padding:1px 6px;border-radius:4px;font-size:.7rem;font-weight:600\">📖 ${citationCount} cit.</span>`\n      : '';\n\n    // Build action links\n    const links = [];\n    if(doiUrl) links.push(`<a href=\"${escapeHtml(doiUrl)}\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" style=\"color:var(--accent-2);text-decoration:none;font-size:.78rem\">DOI ↗</a>`);\n    if(arxivId) links.push(`<a href=\"https://arxiv.org/abs/${escapeHtml(arxivId)}\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" style=\"color:var(--accent-2);text-decoration:none;font-size:.78rem\">arXiv ↗</a>`);\n    if(pdfUrl) links.push(`<a href=\"${escapeHtml(pdfUrl)}\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" style=\"color:var(--tier2);text-decoration:none;font-size:.78rem\">PDF ↗</a>`);\n    if(paperUrl && !doiUrl && !arxivId) links.push(`<a href=\"${escapeHtml(paperUrl)}\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" style=\"color:var(--accent-2);text-decoration:none;font-size:.78rem\">Link ↗</a>`);\n    const linksHtml = links.length\n      ? `<span style=\"display:inline-flex;gap:.7rem;align-items:center\">${links.join('')}</span>`\n      : '';\n\n    // Abstract preview (first 240 chars) + expandable full abstract\n    const fullAbstract = abstract || tldr || '';\n    const hasAbstract = fullAbstract.length > 0;\n    const previewLen = 240;\n    const needExpand = fullAbstract.length > previewLen;\n    const previewText = needExpand ? fullAbstract.slice(0, previewLen) + '…' : fullAbstract;\n    const expandId = `paper-abstract-${idx}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`;\n\n    const abstractHtml = hasAbstract\n      ? `<div style=\"margin-top:.45rem;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.82rem;line-height:1.5\">\n           <span class=\"paper-abstract-preview\" data-expand-id=\"${expandId}\">${escapeHtml(previewText)}</span>\n           ${needExpand ? `<button type=\"button\" class=\"paper-expand-btn\" data-expand-id=\"${expandId}\" style=\"background:none;border:none;color:var(--accent-2);cursor:pointer;font-size:.78rem;padding:0 0 0 .35rem;text-decoration:underline\">↓ expand abstract</button>` : ''}\n           <div class=\"paper-abstract-full\" id=\"${expandId}\" style=\"display:none;margin-top:.4rem;color:var(--text);font-size:.82rem;line-height:1.55\">${escapeHtml(fullAbstract)}</div>\n         </div>`\n      : `<div style=\"margin-top:.35rem;color:var(--text-dim);font-size:.78rem;font-style:italic\">No abstract available.</div>`;\n\n    return `\n      <div style=\"background:#0a0f1c;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:8px;padding:.7rem .85rem;${isSeed ? 'border-left:3px solid var(--tier1);' : ''}\">\n        <div style=\"display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;gap:.5rem;margin-bottom:.35rem\">\n          ${seedBadge}\n          ${sourceBadge}\n          ${citeBadge}\n          ${year ? `<span style=\"color:var(--text-dim);font-size:.75rem;font-family:var(--mono)\">${escapeHtml(year)}</span>` : ''}\n          <span style=\"margin-left:auto;display:flex;gap:.6rem;align-items:center\">${linksHtml}</span>\n        </div>\n        <div style=\"color:var(--text);font-size:.88rem;font-weight:500;line-height:1.4\">\n          ${paperUrl ? `<a href=\"${escapeHtml(paperUrl)}\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\" style=\"color:inherit;text-decoration:none\">${escapeHtml(title)}</a>` : escapeHtml(title)}\n        </div>\n        ${authors ? `<div style=\"margin-top:.2rem;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.78rem\">${escapeHtml(authors)}</div>` : ''}\n        ${venue ? `<div style=\"margin-top:.15rem;color:var(--text-dim);font-size:.75rem;font-style:italic\">${escapeHtml(venue)}</div>` : ''}\n        ${abstractHtml}\n        ${reason ? `<div style=\"margin-top:.35rem;color:var(--text-dim);font-size:.72rem;padding-top:.3rem;border-top:1px dashed var(--border)\">↳ ${escapeHtml(reason)}</div>` : ''}\n      </div>\n    `;\n  }\n\n  function renderFieldMetrics(metrics){\n    if(!metrics){\n      return '<div style=\"color:var(--text-dim);font-size:.82rem;padding:.5rem 0\">No citation-graph metrics available.</div>';\n    }\n    const cells = [\n      {label:'Papers', value: metrics.paper_count ?? {label:'Max cites', value: metrics.max_citation_count ?? 0},\n      {label:'arXiv share', value: (metrics.arxiv_coverage_pct ?? 0) + '%'},\n      {label:'Publishers', value: <div style=\"display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fit,minmax(80px,1fr));gap:.4rem;margin:0 0 .85rem\">\n        ${cells.map(c=>`\n          <div style=\"background:#0a0f1c;border:1px solid style=\"font-size:.65rem;color:var(--text-dim);font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.05em;text-transform:uppercase\">${escapeHtml(c.label)}</div>\n            <div style=\"font-family:var(--mono);font-size:.95rem;color:var(--accent-2);font-weight:700;margin-top:.15rem\">${escapeHtml(String(c.value))}</div>\n          </div>\n        `).join('')}\n      </div>\n      ${metrics.top_venues && metrics.top_venues.length ? `\n        <div style=\"margin:.4rem 0 .85rem;color:var(--text-muted);font-size:.78rem\">\n          <strong style=\"display:inline-block;background:rgba(59,130,246,.1);color:var(--accent-2);padding:1px 7px;border-radius:4px;font-size:.72rem;margin:.15rem .25rem 0 0\">${escapeHtml(v)}</span>`).join('')}\n        </div>\n      ` : ''}\n    `;\n  }\n\n  function renderWeeklyStartups(data){\n    const grid = document.getElementById('weekly-startup-grid');\n    if(!grid) return;\n    if(!data || data.startups.length : 0);\n\n      // Try to fetch pool size (best-effort, non-blocking)\n      try {\n        const poolResp = await fetch(base + '/' + WEEKLY_POOL_JSON, {cache: 'no-cache'});\n        if(poolResp.ok){\n          const pool = await poolResp.json();\n          document.getElementById('weekly-total-pool').textContent = Array.isArray(pool) ? pool.length : '—';\n        } else {\n          document.getElementById('weekly-total-pool').textContent = '—';\n        }\n      } catch(e){\n        document.getElementById('weekly-total-pool').textContent = '—';\n      }\n\n      // Compute paper attach rate\n      const startups = data.startups || [];\n      const withPaper = startups.filter(s => s.paper && s.paper.source !== 'unavailable').length;\n      const rate = startups.length ?
